<details>
<summary>Full prompt</summary>

<pre><code>"A photorealistic editorial lifestyle portrait of a young woman in a sophisticated industrial-style loft. She has fair skin, natural facial features, and long auburn-red hair gathered into a relaxed low ponytail, with a few soft strands framing her face. Her expression is calm and thoughtful, with her gaze directed slightly upward and toward the side rather than directly at the camera. She is positioned in a relaxed seated pose on a soft textured rug, with one knee raised naturally and one arm extending comfortably toward the side, creating an elegant editorial composition. Her posture should look natural and balanced.

She is dressed in a light Green sleeveless fitted top and matching wide-leg trousers, creating a monochromatic, refined outfit. The clothing should have realistic fabric texture, natural folds, and subtle stitching details. Keep the styling tasteful and understated, with minimal jewelry and no visible logos or branding.

The setting is a warm industrial loft interior featuring exposed reddish-brown brick walls, large black-framed grid windows, polished wooden flooring, framed artwork, and a tall vintage-style floor mirror. A wooden table with a classic camera and a few simple objects sits toward the side of the room. An elegant contemporary chandelier with warm metallic elements and hanging crystal-like details adds visual interest overhead. The large mirror naturally reflects the woman and part of the room, creating depth and a sophisticated editorial effect.

Use soft natural daylight entering through the large windows, combined with subtle warm ambient interior lighting. Sunlight should create gentle highlights on the subject's hair, clothing, wooden floor, and brick textures while maintaining realistic shadows. The overall atmosphere should feel warm, peaceful, artistic, and upscale.

Camera and style: shot on an 85mm portrait lens, eye-level perspective, shallow but natural depth of field, realistic perspective, soft window light, cinematic color grading, high dynamic range, detailed skin texture, realistic hair strands, accurate fabric textures, natural shadows, professional fashion-editorial photography, subtle film grain, balanced composition, high resolution, photorealistic detail, sophisticated warm-neutral color palette."</code></pre>

</details>

Eastern European woman of twenty, oval face over high flat cheekbones, straight narrow nose, full mouth, light copper brows, porcelain complexion holding one continuous even tone from hairline to collarbone. Irises muted low-chroma grey-green with a dark limbal ring. Micro-asymmetry: left eyelid crease a fraction lower, right mouth corner higher. HAIR: long copper-ginger in loose waves past the chest, off-centre part; in profile swept behind the ear; from behind a rounded crown, low uneven nape hairline, hair splitting over the shoulder blades.
BUILD: slim and long-limbed, narrow squared shoulders, visible collarbones, flat waist, narrow hips with a compact high-set rounded seat and long thigh line.
WARDROBE: pale pink ribbed cropped tee ending above the navel, a small flat-printed cartoon kitten face on the chest; light-blue distressed denim cut-offs, frayed white hem; black hard-shell knee pads on both knees; black padded wrist guards on both forearms; plain white low-top canvas sneakers, white laces and rubber soles, ankles bare.
POSTURE: arms hanging with a gap from the body, weight even on both feet, shoulders squared and dropped back, chin level, expression flat, eyes level to camera — the same stance throughout.
SCENE: neutral grey seamless cyclorama #8A8A8A curving into a wall-to-floor sweep, real depth behind the figure, brightness pooling behind her, darker to the edges. An apple box and a C-stand sit in that depth, out of focus — visible only in the body panels; the head panels melt the background into an even grey field.
LIGHT: large soft key from camera-left at 45°, neutral 5600K, feathered shadow edge; soft fill at 2:1 from a reflector camera-right, open shadows. A thin bright line of light traces the outer edge of the shoulder, jaw and hair, lifting the silhouette clear of the grey — a narrow restrained band rather than a halo, on the hair and cheekbone in the head panels, on the shoulders and outer arm in the body panels. Soft shadow under the feet. Identical light throughout.
LOOK: filmic-neutral — neutral white balance, natural skin free of cast, low-medium saturation, soft white roll-off. 85mm at f/5.6 throughout, the frontal face panel resolving to full detail with individual eyelashes, iris texture and pore structure all crisp over one unbroken even complexion, Kodak Portra 400 grain.
PANELS
1 head, 0° frontal, crown to collarbones, dominant: facial geometry, grey-green irises, one even skin tone.
2 head, 90° right profile, head and neck, small: nose line, jaw angle.
3 head, 180° from behind, head and neck, small: crown, part line, nape hairline.
4 body, 0° frontal, base of the neck to the floor with air below the soles, HEAD ABSENT: pink tee with chest print, bare midriff, denim cut-offs, knee pads, wrist guards, sneakers.
5 body, 180° from behind, base of the neck to the floor with air below the soles, HEAD ABSENT: plain pink ribbed back with straight hem, denim back with two patch pockets over a compact rounded seat, knee-pad straps buckled behind each knee.
INFO: a text block in the upper-left corner, typeset as printed annotation on the backdrop clear of the photographs: "ZORIANA" in uppercase at a clearly readable display size, then three lines of comfortably legible body text, each about a third the height of the name and each running unbroken across a single line: "Height: 176 cm", "Voice: low, quiet, unhurried, chest-resonant, flat-toned, precise, faintly hoarse", "Character: bold, blunt, private, stubborn, unflinching, dry-humoured, restless" — light on the grey, generous letter spacing, clean sans-serif. The expression stays flat and neutral in every panel, the wording above being printed text rather than direction.
LOCK: all five panels the same woman — identical facial geometry, grey-green eyes, hairline, one even unmarked skin tone, hair and build; same sweep, light, grade, grain and optics; identical crop line at the neck, the head appearing solely in panels 1-3. <pre><code>Render a 16:9 prop turnaround reference sheet according to the following specification. Every field describes the finished sheet.
SHEET:
layout: six orthographic views of the SAME object in a clean 3×2 grid on one sheet, generous even spacing, identical object scale in every view, each view captioned below
aspect: 16:9 horizontal, 4K
MEDIUM:
stock: shot on Hasselblad H6D-100c, medium-format digital capture
artifact: fine detail resolution, natural micro-contrast across leather grain, chrome and urethane
note: every view is a photographed frame of the physical object, same camera and same lens
OBJECT:
description: single right-foot quad roller skate built on a figure-skating boot, high-top cuff standing well above the ankle, structured toe cap tapering to a rounded point, padded tongue sitting proud of the lacing, a low stacked block heel under a straight-lasted sole, a chromed aluminium plate bolted beneath with two trucks, four wheels and a rubber toe stop forward of the front axle
dimensions: 26 cm long overall, 21 cm tall from ground to cuff edge, 12 cm wide across the axles, wheels 58 mm diameter by 32 mm wide, heel block 4 cm tall
material: white full-grain leather upper over a stiff heel counter, white cotton laces through eight pairs of metal eyelets with two upper hooks, white leather-wrapped heel and sole edge, polished chrome-plated aluminium plate and trucks, cream off-white polyurethane wheels on chrome hubs, cream rubber toe stop on a threaded chrome stem
finish: leather satin with a fine pebble grain and a soft sheen breaking over the toe cap, lacquered heel and sole edge slightly glossier than the upper, chrome plate mirror-bright and picking up the beige ground in its reflection, wheel urethane semi-matte with faint mould seams, toe stop rubber matte and dull
wear: a shallow grey scuff on the outer edge of the toe cap, thin street grit worked into the welt seam where the sole meets the upper, a fine scratch across the chrome plate just ahead of the rear axle, road dust settled in the wheel hub spokes and a flattened polished face on the front of the toe stop
anchor: the rubber toe stop — its position tells the skate's orientation in every view
SCENE:
backdrop: clean warm beige seamless studio ground (#EDE4D3) consistent across all six views, soft contact shadow under each view, the object alone on the beige
LIGHT:
key: large diffused softbox from upper-front, soft-edged, raking gently across the leather so the grain and the toe cap curve read as relief
fill: gentle even fill returning off a white card, shadows staying open so the white leather holds separation from the beige
rim: subtle soft edge along the cuff and the chrome plate, lifting the skate off the ground
note: neutral white balance held true, identical lighting in all six views — accurate material reading is the priority
LOOK:
color_grading: neutral catalog grade, the leather reading true clean white against the warmer beige ground, cream urethane holding its faint yellow, chrome staying neutral silver with clean specular pickup
authenticity: 120mm macro at f/13 — the whole skate sharp from cuff edge to wheel tread, leather pebble grain, lace weave, axle nut knurling and urethane mould seams all resolving, broad soft speculars sitting on the chrome
style: shot on Hasselblad H6D-100c, medium-format digital capture, fine detail × studio product photography turnaround × footwear catalog documentation
VIEWS:
front: {camera: eye level, straight on at the toe, note: the toe cap curve and its scuff, the lace bow rising above the tongue, the two front wheels splayed either side of the plate, the cuff opening reading beyond, anchor: toe stop centred low, its flattened face square to the camera, label: "FRONT VIEW"}
back: {camera: eye level, 180° opposite the front, heel to camera, note: the back seam of the boot and the stitched heel counter, the stacked block heel, the rear axle with a wheel standing out to each side, anchor: toe stop concealed behind the boot, label: "BACK VIEW"}
left: {camera: eye level, 90° from the outer side, note: the full boot profile with all eight eyelet pairs and both lace hooks, the plate line under the sole, all four wheels reading in a row, the truck cushion between them, anchor: toe stop at camera-left, projecting ahead of the front wheel, label: "LEFT VIEW"}
right: {camera: eye level, 90° from the inner side, note: the mirrored boot profile with the tongue edge showing through the lacing, the kingpin nut and both axle nuts, the chrome plate scratch ahead of the rear axle, anchor: toe stop at camera-right, projecting ahead of the front wheel, label: "RIGHT VIEW"}
top: {camera: directly above looking straight down the vertical axis — a bird's-eye plan view onto the cuff opening with the boot falling away below, note: the open collar and the padded tongue inside it, the lacing running in a foreshortened ladder toward the toe, the boot outline narrowing over the toe cap, wheel edges appearing just outside that outline at both ends, anchor: toe stop foreshortened to a small pale disc beyond the toe, label: "TOP VIEW"}
bottom: {camera: directly below looking straight up the vertical axis — an underside plan view onto the plate with the boot receding above, note: the chrome plate spine running heel to toe, four urethane treads with their contact wear, the axle nuts, the leather sole and heel block visible between the trucks, anchor: toe stop reading as a flat cream circle on its chrome stem at the toe end, label: "BOTTOM VIEW"}
SHEET_GRAPHICS:
title: "WHITE QUAD ROLLER SKATE" top-left in bold condensed sans-serif, dark charcoal on the beige ground
sub_label: "PROP TURNAROUND — 6 VIEWS" beneath the title in thin uppercase sans-serif
captions: small uppercase sans-serif beneath each view, exactly as written in VIEWS labels
text_rendering: every letterform razor-sharp and fully legible at 4K, each glyph holding its exact designed shape, clean closed counters
CONSISTENCY_LOCK: all six views are the exact same single skate — identical proportions, white leather upper, chrome plate, cream wheels and toe stop, with the toe cap scuff, welt grit, plate scratch and hub dust reproduced in the same position on the object. On the spacious wooden porch, a stylish female traveler relaxes in a rustic rocking chair wrapped in a chunky cream knit blanket. A soft sheepskin rug, knitted pillows, and woven blankets add warmth and texture.

Beyond the porch, towering snow-covered pine trees surround the cabin, with majestic snow-capped mountains glowing under the first rays of sunrise. Gentle snowflakes drift through the air while untouched footprints disappear into the fresh powder. The atmosphere feels calm, magical, and completely secluded.

The floor reveals worn tiles adorned with a black, white, and off-white geometric floral star pattern. A moody, sophisticated atmosphere envelops the scene, defined by a desaturated monochromatic palette punctuated by warm golden accents. Highly directional, hard artificial light cascades from a grid of pristine recessed overhead spotlights that trail off into an infinite reflection in the mirrored ceiling. This dramatic top lighting creates a high-contrast environment devoid of ambient fill, casting harsh, short, and deep black shadows under the man's brow, nose, jawline, and collar, while producing striking specular highlights across his forehead, the bridge of his nose, his cheekbones, and the glossy phone case. Rendered as tack-sharp, realistic digital photography with a subtle touch of digital noise, the image captures this mirror selfie aesthetic through a 24mm equivalent lens at f/2.8, 1/60s, and ISO 800 to maintain a deep depth of field across the reflections. The aesthetic is finalized with crushed blacks, slight overall desaturation, enhanced local contrast on the facial and fabric textures, and a warm golden color balance in the highlights, all flawlessly framed in a 9:16 aspect ratio.</code></pre>

</details>

<details>

<pre><code>Create an ultra-photorealistic candid late-night snapshot of a stylish young woman inside a brightly lit 24-hour convenience store. She is browsing the refrigerated drinks aisle with a small shopping basket overflowing with colorful snacks, bottled beverages, fruit, and convenience-store treats. She wears an oversized vintage graphic T-shirt, relaxed denim shorts, chunky sneakers, slim black sunglasses, a silver bracelet, and a few minimalist rings.
She has naturally beautiful features, expressive eyes, healthy skin with realistic texture, and loosely styled dark hair with a few strands framing her face. As she notices the camera, she playfully turns over her shoulder and makes a subtle kissing-face expression, creating the feeling of an unexpected moment between friends rather than a posed photoshoot.
The convenience store is filled with glowing refrigerator doors, vibrant product packaging, handwritten price labels, stacked displays, and fluorescent ceiling lights that create a distinctly late-night atmosphere. Small details like shopping baskets, promotional signs, and reflections on the glass add realism.
Capture the image as though it were taken with a smartphone using a harsh on-camera flash. Embrace strong shadows, blown highlights, uneven exposure, slight wide-angle lens distortion, subtle motion blur, imperfect focus, visible digital grain, and natural sensor noise. The composition should feel accidental, with a slight Dutch angle, awkward cropping, off-center framing, and the subject caught mid-turn. Avoid polished beauty retouching or flawless studio lighting—retain authentic skin texture, flyaway hairs, and realistic imperfections. The final image should evoke the nostalgic, messy charm of an unplanned late-night convenience-store stop captured in a single spontaneous flash photograph.</code></pre>

- **ImgLume record:** [1932](https://imglume.com/prompt/1932)
- **Source media:** [1](https://cdn.imglume.com/prompts/byradar/9d60f66a-59f6-4dde-952e-f4cb28f6ac6c/ce2236c0-3961-4da1-8701-30c192a10c48-1785480371837 "Image prompt preview") · [2](https://cdn.imglume.com/prompts/byradar/9d60f66a-59f6-4dde-952e-f4cb28f6ac6c/c6fdb8b7-e434-490f-947b-6f8eae225a6f-1785480371837 "Image prompt preview")
- **Rights:** prompt text and source media `NOASSERTION`; ImgLume curation `CC-BY-4.0`

<details>
<summary>Full prompt</summary>

<pre><code>Use ONLY the face from the original photo: facial features, proportions, natural asymmetry, hair color and texture, and skin tone. Keep the face out of focus; recognizability should be preserved only through the head silhouette, face shape, and hair color.

Completely ignore everything else from the original photo.

Focus inversion. The camera is focused on a field of daisies between small green hills. Thousands of daisies, tall grass, a winding path, and soft rays of sunlight are captured in perfect sharpness. The landscape is the main subject of the frame.

In the foreground, a girl is very close to the camera and heavily blurred. She unexpectedly leans down to pick a single daisy, and at that moment the wind blows her hair across her face. One hand holds the flower stem while the other instinctively moves her hair away. A subtle smile can be perceived through the blur. She is completely absorbed in the moment and unaware that she is being photographed.

She is wearing a fitted white bodysuit and high-waisted blue denim shorts.

The composition is spontaneous and candid: the girl occupies less than a quarter of the frame and is partially cropped by the edge of the image. Most of the space is occupied by the field, hills, and sky. The horizon is slightly tilted, as if shot handheld.

Bright summer daylight. The distant background is perfectly sharp while the foreground is softly blurred. Smartphone photography, dreamlike memory, subtle digital grain, natural colors, no HDR, no retouching, no text, and no watermarks.

–ar 4:5 –raw</code></pre>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Full prompt</summary>

<pre><code>Use the exact same face from the reference image and generate a breathtakingly realistic luxurious swimming pool portrait — the kind of image that looks like it was actually shot on location at a five-star resort by a world-class photographer. Every single element grounded in complete photographic realism.

The subject is in a stunning infinity or resort pool — upper body and face above the water, arms resting naturally on the pool edge or floating at the surface, body submerged from the chest down with the water completely clear and turquoise. Completely natural and relaxed — the specific body language of someone genuinely enjoying a luxury pool on a perfect afternoon, not posing for a camera. Head slightly above the water surface, a natural relaxed expression — eyes soft and warm, a quiet genuine smile or completely peaceful neutral expression, completely at ease. Hair wet and naturally slicked back or floating at the water surface around the shoulders — the specific way hair actually behaves when wet, no styling, no perfection.

The pool itself is a masterpiece of luxury resort photography — the water an impossibly beautiful deep turquoise-blue, completely transparent, the pool floor and walls visible beneath the surface in lighter aquamarine tones. Natural light refracting through the water creating dancing caustic light patterns across the submerged body and the pool floor below. The pool edge is elegant — smooth white or travertine stone coping, the material texture visible at the waterline. An infinity edge visible in the background where the pool water appears to merge seamlessly with the horizon beyond — ocean or mountain vista extending infinitely behind, the classic luxury resort infinity pool composition.

The water surface around the subject is completely realistic — gentle natural ripples radiating outward from where the body meets the water, the specific circular wave patterns of real water disturbed by a real person. Water droplets on the shoulders and face catching the sunlight as sharp individual specular highlights. The waterline on the skin showing the specific way water clings to real skin — a thin film, surface tension at the boundary, the skin just above the waterline slightly wet and glistening.

The resort environment surrounding the pool is luxurious and completely real — smooth stone pool deck visible beyond the edge, a folded white resort towel on a sunlounger just visible at the frame edge, perhaps a half-drunk glass of water or fresh coconut on the pool ledge nearby. Tropical or Mediterranean landscape suggested in the soft focus background — lush green palm fronds or cypress trees, white resort architecture, the blue of an ocean or the green of mountains at the horizon beyond the infinity edge.

Lighting is natural afternoon sun — direct warm sunlight from slightly above creating the specific bright open outdoor lighting of a Mediterranean or tropical afternoon. The sun catching the water surface and creating thousands of individual light sparkle reflections across the entire water surface — each one a sharp bright specular highlight. The light on the face warm and direct — natural outdoor sun portrait lighting with real shadows under the brow and nose, real catchlights from the open sky in the eyes, the specific quality of sunlight on wet skin that makes it glow and glisten simultaneously. Natural reflections from the turquoise water throwing subtle blue-green reflected light upward onto the underside of the face and chin.

Skin rendered at full photorealistic obsessive detail — every pore visible, individual water droplets sitting on the skin surface each as a perfect tiny lens, the specific texture of wet skin versus dry skin visible simultaneously across different facial zones. Natural sun-kissed warmth to the skin tone. The eyes catching real outdoor sky catchlights — bright, natural, alive.</code></pre>

</details>

## Realistic full-body portrait with cute chibi wall illustration

- **Original source:** [@Ciri_ai on X](https://x.com/Ciri_ai/status/2082479122312581372)
- **Recommended model:** `gptimage`
- **Source model(s):** `other`
- **Categories:** `character`, `photography`

- **Source media:** [1](https://cdn.imglume.com/prompts/byradar/6fe71f27-435d-40b2-9909-90b69c166e66/36e0327d-4dfc-4d09-a55b-1fc2b2f65d1b-1785389097063 "Image prompt preview") · [2](https://cdn.imglume.com/prompts/byradar/6fe71f27-435d-40b2-9909-90b69c166e66/e0a71850-f211-4b50-a9cc-128479e27a6d-1785389097063 "Image prompt preview")
- **Rights:** prompt text and source media `NOASSERTION`; ImgLume curation `CC-BY-4.0`

<details>
<summary>Full prompt</summary>

<pre><code>Use the uploaded character reference image as the strict identity and outfit reference.

Preserve the reference character’s:
- face identity
- facial proportions
- eye shape
- nose
- lips
- skin tone
- hairstyle
- hair color
- visible hair accessories
- overall recognizable vibe
- outfit and styling shown in the reference image

Do not hardcode any specific character traits that are not present in the uploaded reference.
All identity, hairstyle, accessories, clothing, colors, and styling details must be inferred directly from the uploaded reference image.

Create a high-quality mixed-style vertical portrait showing:
1. a realistic full-body version of the uploaded character/person
2. a cute colorful 2D chibi illustration version of the same character/person drawn on the wall beside them

Core concept:
The real person is posing cutely beside a large colorful chibi wall illustration of themselves.
The real person and the illustrated chibi version are doing a similar playful pose together.
The chibi illustration should look like a cute hand-drawn anime-style wall art version of the same person, not a separate real person.
The mood should feel cute, playful, bright, trendy, charming, and social-media-friendly.

Main style direction:
This is not a black doodle-shadow concept.
This is a realistic person + colorful cute chibi wall illustration concept.
The wall illustration should look like a polished but hand-drawn 2D anime/chibi drawing placed directly on the wall.

Real person:
- realistic human photography
- full-body or nearly full-body framing
- same recognizable identity as the uploaded reference
- same outfit style and key visible details from the uploaded reference
- natural photogenic pose
- cute, playful, slightly shy, or mischievous expression
- the person should actively perform a cute pose that matches the wall illustration
- do not make the person stand stiffly
- do not replace the outfit with unrelated fashion

Real person pose:
Choose one cute playful pose that works well beside a wall illustration:
- both hands near cheeks like cute paws
- one leg lifted slightly
- playful idol-like pose
- shy but cute pose
- tiny heart gesture
- playful bounce pose
- leaning slightly with a sweet smile
- cute “nyan” pose with hands curled near the face
- soft jump-like pose while still realistic and balanced

The pose should feel natural for the real person, not exaggerated or physically awkward.

Chibi wall illustration:
- the illustration must be a cute colorful 2D chibi version of the same uploaded person
- it should be drawn directly on the wall beside the real person
- not a realistic second person
- not a 3D character
- not a black shadow
- not a simple stick figure
- not a random anime character
- not a separate unrelated girl
- it should clearly resemble the real person through hairstyle silhouette, hair color, accessories, outfit silhouette, color palette, and overall vibe
- use large expressive eyes, rounded cute face, small body proportions, and soft anime-style coloring
- the chibi should perform a more exaggerated cute version of the real person’s pose
- the chibi may look like it is happily jumping, posing, or floating slightly as part of the wall illustration

Wall illustration details:
- soft clean linework
- warm pastel colors
- cute anime/chibi rendering
- light hand-drawn texture
- subtle shading
- charming expressive face
- same outfit impression as the real person
- same hairstyle and accessory cues if visible in the reference
- playful motion lines around the chibi
- small hearts, stars, sparkles, soft doodle accents, or tiny decorative marks around the illustration

Interaction:
The real person and the chibi illustration should feel connected.
They should share the same pose idea, but not match perfectly.
The real person performs a realistic cute pose.
The chibi performs a more exaggerated, adorable, anime-like version.
The connection must be instantly readable, like a fun photo taken beside a personalized wall illustration.

Composition:
- vertical 4:5 or 9:16
- simple clean indoor wall
- real person on one side
- colorful chibi wall illustration on the other side
- both figures visible in full-body or nearly full-body framing
- chibi illustration roughly similar height to the real person or slightly larger
- enough negative space around both figures
- keep the wall illustration fully visible
- avoid cropping the real person’s feet or the chibi’s body

Background:
- clean white, cream, pale beige, or light gray wall
- simple floor
- minimal indoor studio or clean room setting
- soft natural light
- uncluttered background
- no TikTok UI, no social media interface, no text overlay

Lighting:
- soft natural studio lighting
- bright, clean, warm, and flattering
- realistic shadow under the real person
- wall illustration should look naturally placed on the wall
- no harsh shadows
- no dramatic dark lighting

Style quality:
- realistic human photography
- colorful 2D chibi wall illustration
- cute anime-style wall art
- strong identity resemblance
- outfit and styling faithfully based on the uploaded reference
- playful matching pose
- soft pastel mood
- social-media-friendly
- clean composition
- high detail
- no obvious AI artifacts

Negative prompt:
black doodle shadow, black line-art only, normal shadow, realistic second person, 3D character, random anime girl, unrelated chibi, weak resemblance, different hairstyle, different outfit, outfit change unrelated to reference, hardcoded traits not in reference, messy wall, cluttered background, TikTok UI, social media interface, text, caption, handwritten text, watermark, logo, cropped feet, cropped chibi, stiff pose, boring standing pose, aggressive pose, horror mood, creepy illustration, distorted body, extra limbs, extra fingers, bad hands, bad anatomy, pasted face, frozen expression, overly artificial AI face</code></pre>

</details>

- **Rights:** prompt text and source media `NOASSERTION`; ImgLume curation `CC-BY-4.0`

<details>
<summary>Full prompt</summary>

<pre><code>A 3x3 photo grid presented as a single image on a dark grey background. Each of the nine grid cells contains a different portrait of the same subject from the uploaded image, maintaining the perfect character consistency, outfit and pose across all panels. Below each individual portrait, a clean sans-serif text label with a number and name is clearly displayed.
The grid layout and lighting setups are:
- Top row, left to right:
Loop Lighting (Flattering 3/4 light with a small loop of shadow from the nose onto the cheek)
Hardlight Frontal Flash (direct front facing, harsh flashlight with deep frontal shadows)
Split Lighting (dramatic light on only one side of the face, leaving the other side completely dark)
- Middle row, left to right:
Butterfly Lighting (classic symmetrical light from the front, slightly high, with a small shadow under the nose)
Gobo Pattern (Venetian Blind)' (light projected through Venetian blinds, creating linear shadows across the face and clothes, the shadow can also be scene on the background)
Under-Lighting (Uplight)' (light coming from below, creating reverse shadows and a moody look)
- Bottom row, left to right:
Golden Hour / Warm Light' (bathed in soft, golden, warm light from a side angle)
Dramatic Spotlight (hard, narrow circular beam of light, with a deep chiascuro effect and crushed blacks)
Neutral Kelvin Rim Light' (intense backlighting with no front fill, creating a distinct glowing outline around the hair and shoulders, leaving the face in darkness, against a black background).
The perspective and features remain identical in all panels, with only the specified lighting changing to match the label.</code></pre>

</details>

<details>

<pre><code>两位年轻成年东亚女性并排坐在明亮极简公寓厨房的小型木质台面上，画面为完整坐姿全身构图，人物位于中央。左侧女性身体略朝画面右侧，右腿屈膝抬起，左腿自然向下伸展，赤脚悬空；她穿纯白色细肩带三角比基尼，侧腰系带清晰。右侧女性正面略朝左坐着，双腿自然垂下，赤脚悬空，穿深海军蓝色简洁连体泳衣，宽肩带、低圆领。

两人彼此对视，不看镜头，表情安静、自然、略带亲近感。左侧女性的右手轻扶右侧女性的左上臂，左手自然放在台面或两人之间；右侧女性左手放在两人之间，右手撑在台面边缘。两人的手臂、手指和腿部关系必须清晰，不交叉穿模。

左侧女性为深黑色长直发，带整齐薄刘海；右侧女性为深棕黑色高马尾，同样有轻薄刘海。两人均为自然淡妆，白皙中性肤色，保留真实毛孔、细小绒毛、轻微肤色差异和自然高光，不要塑料磨皮。

环境为冷白色小厨房或公寓角落：浅灰白墙面、白色柜体、浅木色台面。画面左侧有银白色小冰箱，上方放一台白色意式咖啡机和小型绿植；右侧为大面积白色纱帘和明亮窗光。地面浅灰，空间干净、无杂物。

光线为右侧窗户照入的柔和冷调自然漫射光，整体高键、低饱和、低对比，以冷白、浅灰、浅木色、海军蓝和自然肤色为主。摄影风格：50mm镜头，f/3.2，平视机位，中等景深，人物和脚部清晰，背景轻柔虚化，真实泳装、皮肤、木材和金属纹理，轻微ISO 200数码颗粒，日系生活方式摄影，photorealistic。

避免：未成年人特征、裸体、透明泳装、色情动作、夸张身材、双人脸部混淆、额外人物、错误手指、融合手臂、重复腿脚、悬浮肢体、错误脚趾、强HDR、过度锐化、杂乱厨房、文字、水印、Logo。</code></pre>

她拥有精致柔和的鹅蛋脸、清晰但不过分尖锐的下颌线、杏仁形深棕眼睛、自然细眉、纤细鼻梁和浅裸粉色嘴唇。妆容极淡，仅有轻薄底妆、自然睫毛、细眼线、柔和灰棕眼影和轻微腮红。肤色白皙偏暖，保留真实毛孔、细小绒毛、自然肤色变化与柔和高光，禁止塑料磨皮。

深棕黑色长直发自然披散，中分略偏侧，头发顺滑垂落至胸前和背后，右侧发丝覆盖部分肩膀，耳侧与脸颊有少量自然碎发。发丝保留真实重量、细节与轻微不规则感。

她穿浅燕麦色或灰米色无肩带短款抹胸，上衣为柔软细罗纹针织面料，贴合身体，长度至肋骨下方，边缘简洁。下身穿同色宽松亚麻抽绳长裤，低腰或中低腰设计，裤腰有自然褶皱、纽扣和细抽绳；一条细腰绳从腰部斜向交叉环绕，形成简约Y2K细节。整体配色统一、低饱和、自然朴素。

背景为无缝暖白或浅奶油灰墙面，无家具、无装饰。采用柔和自然窗光或大型柔光箱，从画面左前上方照射，右侧使用轻微反光板补光。阴影极浅，整体高键、低对比、低饱和，呈现奶油白、燕麦灰、暖肤色与深棕发色的柔和层次。

摄影风格：85mm人像镜头，f/2，平视略高机位，中浅景深，面部、锁骨和服装纹理清晰，背景均匀柔化，真实皮肤、发丝、针织与亚麻纹理，轻微ISO 200胶片颗粒，Japanese minimalist editorial portrait，soft natural beauty photography，photorealistic。

The natural daylight streams in from the window on the left, illuminating her left side and creating soft highlights on her hair, skin, and shoulders. A contrasting golden sunbeam hits the menu board and the wall on the right. The overall café ambience is softly lit, maintaining rich shadows. The camera is set for an American shot (medium-full shot), shot from a slightly low angle, with focus perfectly sharp on the eyes. 环境为空旷现代极简室内，浅米灰色微水泥墙面与地面，无家具、无装饰，仅利用建筑空间塑造氛围。整体色彩以米白、燕麦灰、浅暖灰、肤色组成低饱和莫兰迪色系。

<details>
<summary>Full prompt</summary>

<pre><code>一位年轻成年东亚女性跪坐在海边沙滩铺开的浅米色毛巾上，画面从头顶拍至小腿附近。人物位于中央偏右，身体略微侧向画面左侧，双膝弯曲跪坐，一条腿向侧前方展开，另一条腿收在身体下方；上半身自然前倾，双臂伸直，双手撑在毛巾上。她正面看向镜头，眼神安静、柔和、略带慵懒感，嘴唇自然微启，没有明显笑容。

她有精致柔和的鹅蛋脸、杏仁形深棕眼睛、自然细眉、纤细鼻梁和浅裸粉色嘴唇。妆容极淡，仅保留自然睫毛、细眼线、柔和腮红和湿润唇色。皮肤白皙偏暖，保留真实毛孔、细小绒毛、轻微肤色变化、自然汗感和阳光高光，禁止塑料磨皮。

深棕黑色长发自然披散，带轻微湿润感和柔软波浪；额前有轻薄碎刘海，部分发丝被海风吹过脸颊、肩膀和胸前，发丝边缘被夕阳照亮。

她穿浅沙色、裸米色细肩带三角比基尼，布料哑光柔软，剪裁简洁，颜色接近肤色但边缘清晰，贴合自然，不透明。泳装和皮肤上带少量细沙与水汽。

背景是安静海滩，左后方为浅蓝绿色海水与轻柔浪花，远处有深色岛屿或山体，右后方为模糊海岸线。太阳位于画面右后方低角度，形成暖金色侧逆光、发丝轮廓光、肩颈和手臂高光，海面出现明亮闪烁光斑。天空为浅灰蓝与奶油金渐变，整体低饱和、低对比、温暖通透。

摄影风格：85mm人像镜头，f/1.8，略低于眼睛的近距离拍摄，浅景深，眼睛清晰，海面柔和虚化，真实沙粒、毛巾、皮肤和泳装纹理，轻微ISO 200胶片颗粒，柔和高光晕染，日系写真与高级生活方式摄影结合，photorealistic。

避免：未成年人特征、裸体、露点、透明泳装、色情动作、CG、动漫、过度磨皮、夸张身体比例、错误手指、畸形腿脚、僵硬姿势、过强HDR、过度锐化、杂乱游客、文字、水印、Logo。</code></pre>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Full prompt</summary>

<pre><code>一位年轻成年东亚女性站在极简白色摄影棚或明亮室内空间中，画面为背面三分之二到大半身构图，人物位于画面中央。身体背对镜头，略微侧向画面左侧，头部低垂并转向左下方，露出柔和侧脸轮廓，眼睛向下看，不直视镜头，神情安静、克制、柔和。

她拥有精致鹅蛋脸，杏仁形深棕眼睛，自然细眉，纤细鼻梁，浅裸粉色嘴唇。妆容极淡，皮肤白皙偏暖，保留真实毛孔、细小绒毛、自然肤色变化和柔和高光，不要塑料磨皮。背部、肩胛骨、腰线自然真实，不夸张。

头发为深黑色，松散盘成略凌乱的高位发髻，头顶蓬松，耳侧、颈后和脸侧有细碎发丝自然垂落，发丝带真实凌乱感。

她穿白色露背上衣，肩部自然滑落，长袖为轻薄半透明网纱或薄纱材质，袖子贴合手臂并在手腕处自然堆叠；背部由白色与浅金色细绳交叉绑带固定，中央有蝴蝶结和垂落绳结，绑带结构清晰但不过度复杂。下身穿白色轻薄高腰裤或裙裤，面料柔软，带细密金色点状装饰和蓝金色孔雀羽毛图案，腰侧有白色抽绳系结。

背景为干净白色或浅灰白无缝墙面，无杂物。光线为柔和自然窗光或高键棚拍漫射光，从左前方照入，阴影极浅，整体低饱和、低对比、奶油白空气感。摄影风格：85mm人像镜头，f/2，浅景深，眼睛和侧脸清晰，真实皮肤与布料纹理，轻微胶片颗粒，photorealistic。

避免： 卡通、CG、插画、过度磨皮、夸张身材、错误手指、畸形手臂、扭曲脊柱、错误绑带结构、过度暴露、强HDR、过度锐化、杂乱背景、文字、水印、Logo。</code></pre>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Full prompt</summary>

<pre><code>一位年轻成年东亚女性坐在深色沙发上，画面为半身到大半身构图，人物位于画面中央偏右。身体侧坐，腰部微微扭转，右肩靠近镜头，头部回转看向镜头，眼神安静直接，嘴唇自然微启，神情柔和、慵懒、略带私密感。左手抬起轻扶头顶或发丝，右手自然撑在沙发坐垫上，姿态放松但有曲线感。

她有柔和精致的鹅蛋脸，杏仁形深棕眼睛，自然细眉，纤细鼻梁，浅裸粉色嘴唇。妆容极淡，仅保留自然睫毛、细眼线和轻微腮红。皮肤白皙偏暖，保留真实毛孔、细小绒毛、自然肤色变化和柔和高光，不要塑料磨皮。

她有深黑棕色长发，自然披散，发量丰富，略带凌乱波浪感，部分发丝落在脸侧、肩膀和胸前，发尾带自然蓬松感。

服装为白色修身长袖露肩短上衣，柔软贴身面料，一侧肩膀自然露出，衣摆位于腰线上方；下身穿浅色柔软居家短裤，带轻微蓝色小碎花或细小图案，腰部有自然褶皱。整体造型清爽、柔和、生活化。

背景是暗调室内休息区或卧室沙发区，黑色沙发与深色靠垫，后方墙面为深灰黑色软包或现代装饰板，隐藏式暖黄色灯带横向发光，形成温暖背景光。左侧和后方大面积暗部保留细节。光线为低照度室内灯光与柔和侧光混合，暖色轮廓光照亮头发边缘，面部有柔和补光。整体低饱和、低对比、暗调电影感，带轻微胶片颗粒。

摄影风格：85mm 人像镜头，f/1.8，浅景深，眼睛和面部清晰，背景柔和虚化，真实光学成像，photorealistic，ultra realistic，natural skin texture。

避免： 卡通、CG、插画、过度磨皮、浓妆、夸张表情、夸张身材、错误手指、畸形手臂、强HDR、过度锐化、杂乱背景、文字、水印、Logo。</code></pre>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Full prompt</summary>

<pre><code>Create a photorealistic 3:4 nighttime street portrait of an exceptionally beautiful adult East Asian woman in her late 20s, standing beside a quiet city road after dark.

She has a refined softly rounded face, warm fair skin, large dark-brown almond-shaped eyes, long eyelashes, softly arched eyebrows, a small straight nose and glossy rose-brown lips. Her expression is warm, confident and approachable, with a gentle closed-mouth smile and direct eye contact with the camera. Add a tiny beauty mark near the side of her nose.

Her very long, straight dark-brown hair is parted slightly to one side and falls over her right shoulder past her waist, with a few soft strands across her forehead. She wears large thin silver hoop earrings and a delicate gold necklace with a small pendant.

She has a naturally voluptuous hourglass figure with a full bust, defined waist and soft realistic proportions. She wears a fitted pale butter-yellow spaghetti-strap dress with a low rounded neckline and opaque stretch fabric. The dress follows her silhouette naturally with subtle folds around the waist.

Frame her from the top of her head to the lower waist, with her torso turned slightly sideways while her face turns back toward the camera. The camera is positioned close to her at eye level with a mild three-quarter angle.

The background is a dim, nearly empty city street with a yellow road line running diagonally behind her. Distant streetlights become large creamy white, pale blue and muted red bokeh circles. Warm amber light softly illuminates her face, collarbones and shoulders from the front-left, while the opposite side falls into gentle shadow. Add a subtle golden rim light along her hair.

Shot on a full-frame camera with an 85mm lens at f/1.4, extremely shallow depth of field, cinematic warm color grading, realistic skin pores, natural highlights, slight high-ISO grain and authentic nighttime photography. No anime, no plastic skin, no excessive beauty filter, no distorted anatomy, no transparent clothing, no text, logo, watermark or rounded image corners.</code></pre>

</details>

<pre><code>一位年轻成年东亚女性坐在明亮的厨房餐桌旁，近景到半身构图，人物位于画面中央略偏右。她身体微微前倾，双臂自然放在木质餐桌上，头部轻轻向画面右侧倾斜，正面看向镜头，眼神柔和亲近，嘴唇微启，带轻微自然微笑。整体气质清新、安静、温柔、生活化。

她有柔和精致的鹅蛋脸，杏仁形深棕眼睛，自然细眉，纤细鼻梁，浅珊瑚粉色嘴唇。妆容极淡，仅保留自然睫毛、轻微眼线、柔和腮红和健康肤色。皮肤白皙偏暖，保留真实毛孔、细小绒毛、自然肤色变化和柔和高光，不要塑料磨皮。

头发为深棕黑色，松散盘成低位凌乱发髻，耳侧、额前、颈侧有大量自然碎发和细长发丝，部分发丝垂落在脸颊、肩膀和胸前，带轻微湿润或空气感。

她穿白色露肩连衣裙或露肩上衣，柔软棉质或轻薄皱感面料，胸口有细密荷叶边和松紧褶皱，肩部自然露出，袖口蓬松，整体干净纯白、柔和、含蓄。佩戴细银色项链，心形小吊坠；手腕佩戴细手链。

背景是明亮温暖的厨房，后方有大面积白色窗户，窗外绿植虚化，窗台上有玻璃罐、饼干罐和厨房小物。右侧桌面有透明玻璃水果碗，装有红苹果、橙子；前景木质砧板上有切开的红心葡萄柚和水果片。左后方有水槽、浅色橱柜和干净台面。

光线为强烈但柔和的自然窗光，从后方和左侧照入，形成高键曝光、轻微逆光、柔和高光晕染和空气感。整体低饱和、低对比，以奶油白、浅木色、柔黄、苹果红、橙色和自然肤色为主。摄影风格：85mm人像镜头，f/1.8，浅景深，眼睛和面部清晰，背景柔和虚化，轻微胶片颗粒，photorealistic，ultra realistic，natural skin texture。

避免： 卡通、CG、插画、过度磨皮、浓妆、夸张表情、错误手指、畸形手臂、过曝丢失细节、强HDR、过度锐化、杂乱厨房、文字、水印、Logo。</code></pre>
